function ellipsoid(group,x,y,z,rx,ry,rz,mat,segments=32,rings=24){const m=new ae(new Mn(1,segments,rings),mat);m.position.set(x,y,z);m.scale.set(rx,ry,rz);group.add(m);return m}
function limb(group,a,b,r1,r2,mat,segments=24){const start=new I(...a),end=new I(...b),direction=end.clone().sub(start);const m=new ae(new dn(r2,r1,direction.length(),segments,3),mat);m.position.copy(start.add(end).multiplyScalar(.5));m.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(new I(0,1,0),direction.normalize());group.add(m);return m}
function surfaceMesh(group,rows,columns,point,mat){const positions=[],uvs=[],indices=[];for(let y=0;y<=rows;y++)for(let x=0;x<=columns;x++){positions.push(...point(x/columns,y/rows));uvs.push(x/columns,y/rows)}for(let y=0;y<rows;y++)for(let x=0;x<columns;x++){const a=y*(columns+1)+x,b=a+columns+1;indices.push(a,b,a+1,b,b+1,a+1)}const g=new rn();g.setAttribute('position',new sn(new Float32Array(positions),3));g.setAttribute('uv',new sn(new Float32Array(uvs),2));g.setIndex(indices);g.computeVertexNormals();const m=new ae(g,mat);group.add(m);return m}
function hairStrand(group,points,radius,mat){const positions=[],uvs=[],indices=[],radial=8;for(let j=0;j<points.length;j++){const p=new I(...points[j]),before=new I(...points[Math.max(0,j-1)]),after=new I(...points[Math.min(points.length-1,j+1)]),tangent=after.sub(before).normalize();let right=new I(0,1,0).cross(tangent).normalize();if(right.lengthSq()<.01)right=new I(1,0,0);const up=tangent.clone().cross(right).normalize();for(let i=0;i<=radial;i++){const a=i/radial*TAU,q=p.clone().addScaledVector(right,Math.cos(a)*radius).addScaledVector(up,Math.sin(a)*radius);positions.push(q.x,q.y,q.z);uvs.push(i/radial,j/(points.length-1))}}
 for(let j=0;j<points.length-1;j++)for(let i=0;i<radial;i++){const a=j*(radial+1)+i,b=a+radial+1;indices.push(a,a+1,b,b,a+1,b+1)}const g=new rn();g.setAttribute('position',new sn(new Float32Array(positions),3));g.setAttribute('uv',new sn(new Float32Array(uvs),2));g.setIndex(indices);g.computeVertexNormals();group.add(new ae(g,mat));}

// One mesh per material per detail tier, retaining smooth normals and UVs.
function batchDollPieces(group){group.updateMatrixWorld(true);const inverse=group.matrixWorld.clone().invert(),batches=new Map(),source=[];group.traverse(m=>{if(!m.isMesh)return;source.push(m);const g=m.geometry.clone().applyMatrix4(inverse.clone().multiply(m.matrixWorld));const flat=g.index?g.toNonIndexed():g;const key=m.material.uuid;let b=batches.get(key);if(!b){b={mat:m.material,positions:[],normals:[],uvs:[],colours:[]};batches.set(key,b)}b.positions.push(flat.attributes.position.array);b.normals.push(flat.attributes.normal.array);if(flat.attributes.uv)b.uvs.push(flat.attributes.uv.array);if(m.material.vertexColors)b.colours.push(flat.attributes.color?.array||new Float32Array(flat.attributes.position.count*3).fill(1));flat!==g&&flat.dispose();g.dispose()});const join=arrays=>{const out=new Float32Array(arrays.reduce((n,a)=>n+a.length,0));let offset=0;for(const a of arrays){out.set(a,offset);offset+=a.length}return out};for(const m of source){m.parent.remove(m);m.geometry.dispose()}for(const b of batches.values()){const geometry=new rn();geometry.setAttribute('position',new sn(join(b.positions),3));geometry.setAttribute('normal',new sn(join(b.normals),3));geometry.setAttribute('uv',new sn(join(b.uvs),2));if(b.colours.length)geometry.setAttribute('color',new sn(join(b.colours),3));geometry.computeBoundingSphere();const m=new ae(geometry,b.mat);m.receiveShadow=false;group.add(m)}}
const dollHeadRig=new It(),dollHeadMain=new It(),dollHeadDetails=new It();dollHeadRig.position.y=1;dollHeadRig.add(dollHeadMain,dollHeadDetails);
for(const [parent,keep,to]of [[dollBody,bodyBeforeHead,dollHeadMain],[dollFineDetails,fineBeforeHead,dollHeadDetails]])for(const m of [...parent.children])if(!keep.has(m)){to.add(m);m.position.y-=1;}
for(const lid of dollEyelids){dollHeadRig.add(lid);lid.position.y-=1;}

}

function enhanceNpc(npc,name){
 const head=npc.userData.head,skin=head.children[0].material,shirt=npc.children[0].material,details=new It();npc.add(details);npcDetailGroups.push(details);

 for(let i=0;i<hairPositions.count;i++){const x=hairPositions.getX(i),y=hairPositions.getY(i),z=hairPositions.getZ(i),radius=Math.hypot(x,y,z);if(radius<.001)continue;const phi=Math.atan2(z,x),front=(Math.sin(phi)+1)/2,theta=Math.acos(clamp(y/radius,-1,1))*(1-.40*front*front);hairPositions.setXYZ(i,Math.cos(phi)*Math.sin(theta)*radius,Math.cos(theta)*radius,Math.sin(phi)*Math.sin(theta)*radius)}hairPositions.needsUpdate=true;hair.geometry.computeVertexNormals();

 const seam=material(0x5e6658),shoe=material(0x242a28);
 box(details,0,1.12,.18,.015,.42,.012,seam);

   const arm=npc.children.find(m=>m.isMesh&&m.geometry.parameters.height===.56&&Math.sign(m.position.x)===side),leg=npc.children.find(m=>m.isMesh&&m.geometry.parameters.height===.66&&Math.sign(m.position.x)===side);

   for(const m of [arm,hand,cuff]){shoulder.add(m);m.position.sub(shoulder.position)}

   npc.userData.runRig??={arms:[],legs:[]};npc.userData.runRig.arms.push(shoulder);npc.userData.runRig.legs.push(hip);
  }
 }

}

function throwOldFlash(){
 if(flashBreak)return;state.flags.oldFlashBroken=true;state.flags.flashThrown=true;flashlight=false;
 const origin=ct.localToWorld(new I(.25,-.245,-.49)),dir=ct.getWorldDirection(new I());dir.y=0;if(dir.lengthSq()<.01)dir.set(0,0,-1);dir.normalize();
 let distance=3.4;for(let q=.4;q<3.4;q+=.2)if(collides(origin.x+dir.x*q,origin.z+dir.z*q,.08)){distance=Math.max(.3,q-.25);break;}
 flashBreak={t:0,origin,dir,distance,exploded:false};thrownFlash.visible=true;thrownFlash.position.copy(origin);He.creak();
}
function updateFlashBreak(dt){
 if(!flashBreak)return;const a=flashBreak;a.t+=dt;
 const u=clamp(a.t/.95,0,1);thrownFlash.position.copy(a.origin).addScaledVector(a.dir,a.distance*u);thrownFlash.position.y=Math.max(.18,a.origin.y*(1-u)+.18*u+Math.sin(u*Math.PI)*.68);thrownFlash.rotation.set(u*8,u*5,.3);
 flashStoryLight.position.copy(thrownFlash.position);flashStoryLight.position.y+=.7;
 if(a.t<1.05){flashStoryLight.intensity=27+Math.sin(a.t*43)*6;return;}
 if(!a.exploded){a.exploded=true;state.flags.flashExploded=true;flashSparks.position.copy(thrownFlash.position);flashSparks.position.y=Math.max(.24,flashSparks.position.y);flashSparks.visible=true;thrownFlash.visible=false;He.slam();shake=.4;}

}

function fitDiscoverySunShadow(){
 if(!KALA_LIGHTING_REVISION)return;
 // Include the actual archive/generator roofs as well as the main hall.
 relayRoom.group.updateMatrixWorld(true);discoverySun.shadow.updateMatrices(discoverySun);
 const camera=discoverySun.shadow.camera,inv=camera.matrixWorldInverse;
 let minX=Infinity,minY=Infinity,minZ=Infinity,maxX=-Infinity,maxY=-Infinity,maxZ=-Infinity;
 relayRoom.group.traverse(mesh=>{if(!mesh.isMesh||!mesh.castShadow)return;for(let q=mesh;q&&q!==relayRoom.group;q=q.parent)if(!q.visible)return;
  mesh.geometry.computeBoundingBox();const b=mesh.geometry.boundingBox;
  for(const x of [b.min.x,b.max.x])for(const y of [b.min.y,b.max.y])for(const z of [b.min.z,b.max.z]){const v=new I(x,y,z).applyMatrix4(mesh.matrixWorld).applyMatrix4(inv);minX=Math.min(minX,v.x);maxX=Math.max(maxX,v.x);minY=Math.min(minY,v.y);maxY=Math.max(maxY,v.y);minZ=Math.min(minZ,v.z);maxZ=Math.max(maxZ,v.z);}
 });
 camera.left=minX-.5;camera.right=maxX+.5;camera.bottom=minY-.5;camera.top=maxY+.5;camera.near=Math.max(.1,-maxZ-.5);camera.far=-minZ+.5;camera.updateProjectionMatrix();discoverySun.shadow.needsUpdate=true;
}

const beamPositions=[],beamUvs=[];

for(let i=0;i<4;i++){const j=(i+1)%4,a=beamCorner(i,roofY-.15),b=beamCorner(j,roofY-.15),c=beamCorner(j,.04),d=beamCorner(i,.04);for(const [v,uv]of [[a,[0,1]],[b,[1,1]],[c,[1,0]],[a,[0,1]],[c,[1,0]],[d,[0,0]]]){beamPositions.push(v.x,v.y,v.z);beamUvs.push(...uv);}}
const beamGeometry=new rn();beamGeometry.setAttribute('position',new Dt(beamPositions,3));beamGeometry.setAttribute('uv',new Dt(beamUvs,2));

// Ray/triangle tests use the actual procedural meshes. The bundled Three build
// does not include Raycaster; these local tests avoid another engine dependency.
function rayBoxDistance(o,d,b){let lo=0,hi=Infinity;for(const axis of ['x','y','z']){if(Math.abs(d[axis])<1e-8){if(o[axis]<b.min[axis]||o[axis]>b.max[axis])return null}else{let a=(b.min[axis]-o[axis])/d[axis],c=(b.max[axis]-o[axis])/d[axis];if(a>c)[a,c]=[c,a];lo=Math.max(lo,a);hi=Math.min(hi,c);if(hi<lo)return null}}return lo}
function castRoomSurface(origin,dir){const room=rooms[state.room];if(!room)return null;room.group.updateWorldMatrix(true,true);let best=null,bestDistance=Infinity;const a=new I(),b=new I(),c=new I(),e1=new I(),e2=new I(),p=new I(),q=new I(),s=new I();
 const visit=(m,visible)=>{visible=visible&&m.visible;if(!visible||m.userData.noGrip)return;if(m.isMesh&&m.geometry?.attributes.position){const g=m.geometry;if(!g.boundingBox)g.computeBoundingBox();const inv=m.matrixWorld.clone().invert(),o=origin.clone().applyMatrix4(inv),d=dir.clone().transformDirection(inv),entry=rayBoxDistance(o,d,g.boundingBox);if(entry!==null){const broad=o.clone().addScaledVector(d,entry).applyMatrix4(m.matrixWorld).distanceTo(origin);if(broad<=bestDistance+.02){const pos=g.attributes.position,idx=g.index,count=idx?idx.count:pos.count;for(let i=0;i<count;i+=3){a.fromBufferAttribute(pos,idx?idx.getX(i):i);b.fromBufferAttribute(pos,idx?idx.getX(i+1):i+1);c.fromBufferAttribute(pos,idx?idx.getX(i+2):i+2);e1.subVectors(b,a);e2.subVectors(c,a);p.crossVectors(d,e2);const det=e1.dot(p);if(Math.abs(det)<1e-8)continue;const inverse=1/det;s.subVectors(o,a);const u=inverse*s.dot(p);if(u<0||u>1)continue;q.crossVectors(s,e1);const v=inverse*d.dot(q);if(v<0||u+v>1)continue;const t=inverse*e2.dot(q);if(t<.04)continue;const point=o.clone().addScaledVector(d,t).applyMatrix4(m.matrixWorld),distance=point.distanceTo(origin);if(distance<bestDistance){bestDistance=distance;best={point,distance,mesh:m}}}}}}for(const child of m.children)visit(child,visible)};visit(room.group,true);return best;
}
function clearToolLine(point){const origin=ct.getWorldPosition(new I()),distance=point.distanceTo(origin),hit=castRoomSurface(origin,point.clone().sub(origin).normalize());return !hit||hit.distance>=distance-.75}
function toolCandidates(){const candidates=toolTargets.filter(t=>t.room===state.room&&t.available()&&!currentFight);
 // E interactions stay local. Only authored claw targets accept R at distance.
 for(const b of bolts)candidates.push({point:()=>b.mesh.position.clone(),radius:.42,label:b.kind==='hand'?'TANGKAP TANGAN':'TANGKAP LENDIR',bolt:b,onGrab:()=>{const index=bolts.indexOf(b);if(index>=0){bolts.splice(index,1);disposeCombatNode(b.mesh);beamHeat=Math.max(0,beamHeat-.28);state.sanity=clamp(state.sanity+3,0,100);He.click(.2)}}});
 for(const h of smallerHands)if(!h.disabled)candidates.push({point:()=>h.mesh.position.clone().add(new I(0,.16,0)),radius:.55,label:'LEPAS TANGAN',hand:h,onGrab:()=>disableSmallHand(h)});
 if(currentFight?.kind==='tumbal'){if(currentFight.shield)for(const seal of finalSeals)if(seal.hp>0)candidates.push({point:()=>seal.mesh.position.clone().add(new I(0,1.43,0)),radius:.48,label:'LILIN '+['I','II','III'][seal.index]+' · '+seal.hp+'/3',onGrab:()=>breakFinalSeal(seal)});for(const minion of ashMinions)if(minion.active)candidates.push({point:()=>minion.mesh.position.clone().add(new I(0,1.05,0)),radius:.52,label:'JURU ABU · '+Math.ceil(minion.hp)+' HP',onGrab:()=>damageAshMinion(minion,6)});}
 return candidates;
}
function raycastToolTargets(){if(!state.flags.lenteraOwned||overlay||paused||sequence||fade||daisyAnimation)return null;const origin=ct.getWorldPosition(new I()),dir=ct.getWorldDirection(new I()),surface=castRoomSurface(origin,dir);let nearest=null,best=Infinity;
 for(const t of toolCandidates()){const point=t.point(),delta=point.clone().sub(origin),along=delta.dot(dir),sideSq=delta.lengthSq()-along*along;if(along<0||sideSq>t.radius*t.radius)continue;const distance=Math.max(0,along-Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,t.radius*t.radius-sideSq)));if(distance<best&&(!surface||surface.distance>=distance-.75)){best=distance;nearest={target:t,point,distance}}}
 if(nearest)return nearest;if(!surface)return null;const point=surface.point;return {target:{surface:true,persistent:true,label:'TAMBAT PERMUKAAN',point:()=>point.clone(),onGrab:()=>{}},point,distance:surface.distance};
}
function launchToolHand(){if(!state.flags.lenteraOwned){subtitle('Penjepit belum ditemukan.',2);return}if(paused||overlay||sequence||fade||daisyAnimation)return;if(toolReel){toolReel.retractStart=toolReel.phase==='extend'?clamp(toolReel.time/toolReel.duration,0,1):1;toolReel.phase='retract';toolReel.time=0;return}if(toolCooldown>0)return;const hit=raycastToolTargets();if(!hit){toast('');return}toolReel={phase:'extend',time:0,hit,point:hit.point.clone(),duration:clamp(hit.distance/32,.16,1.4),activated:false};heldLentera.claw.group.visible=false;extendedClaw.group.visible=true;reelCable.visible=true;state.flags.lenteraTutorialDone=true;He.creak()}
function updateToolVisual(dt){updateFlashBreak(dt);updateRelayVisual();const active=state.flags.lenteraOwned&&!lenteraTake;carriedCell.visible=!!state.flags.relayCellHeld;toolMats.lens.color.set(flashlight&&lampAvailable()&&beamCooldown<=0?0xc7c6a2:0x374339);const canUse=!['title','ending'].includes(state.room)&&!daisyAnimation&&!devour&&!daisyRescue;heldLentera.root.visible=!!active&&canUse;oldFlashVisual.visible=!active&&!state.flags.oldFlashBroken&&canUse;lenteraPickup.root.visible=!state.flags.lenteraOwned||!!lenteraTake;toolCooldown=Math.max(0,toolCooldown-dt);
 if(heldLentera.root.visible){heldLentera.root.position.y=-.285+Math.sin(now*3)*.003;Wn.position.set(.32,-.19,-.78)}else Wn.position.set(.25,-.245,-.64);
 if(toolReel){const r=toolReel;r.time+=dt;const origin=heldLentera.root.localToWorld(new I(0,0,-.235));if(r.phase==='extend'&&(!r.hit.target.bolt||bolts.includes(r.hit.target.bolt)))r.point.copy(r.hit.target.point());let t;
  if(r.phase==='extend'){t=clamp(r.time/r.duration,0,1);if(t>=1){r.phase='hold';r.time=0;r.activated=!r.hit.target.bolt||bolts.includes(r.hit.target.bolt);if(r.activated&&!r.hit.target.persistent){r.hit.target.onGrab();if(!toolReel)return}}}
  else if(r.phase==='hold'){t=1;if(!r.hit.target.persistent&&r.time>.12){r.phase='retract';r.time=0}}
  else{t=(r.retractStart??1)*(1-clamp(r.time/.28,0,1));if(t<=0){cancelToolReach();toolCooldown=.25}}
  if(toolReel){const tip=origin.clone().lerp(r.point,t);extendedClaw.group.position.copy(tip);const dir=r.point.clone().sub(origin).normalize();extendedClaw.group.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(new I(0,0,-1),dir);const end=tip.clone().sub(origin);reelCable.position.copy(origin.clone().add(tip).multiplyScalar(.5));reelCable.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(new I(0,1,0),end.clone().normalize());reelCable.scale.y=Math.max(.01,end.length());for(const j of extendedClaw.jaws)j.scale.x=r.phase==='hold'?.66:1}
 }
 toolHintClock-=dt;if(toolHintClock<=0){toolHintClock=.12;toolRayHint=toolReel?null:raycastToolTargets();$('toolTutorial').classList.remove('show')}updateHeldToolPose(dt);
}
function applyToolStory(restore){if(state.room==='loading'&&state.flags.overseerDefeated&&!state.flags.flashBreakComplete)queueAfterFade(flashBreakSequence);if(state.flags.lenteraOwned)state.flags.oldFlashBroken=true;if(state.room==='relay'){state.flags.oldFlashBroken=true;state.flags.flashBreakComplete=true}setDoorOpen('relay',!!state.flags.relaySolved,true)}


// Factory passages extend the existing rooms. The normal movement/collision
// system owns them; there is no teleport until the player reaches the landing.
const factoryStairs={};
function addFactoryStairs(id,next,unlocked,passable){
 const room=rooms[id],g=new It(),z=-room.depth/2;
 g.name='Tangga_'+id;g.position.set(room.cx,0,z);room.group.add(g);
 box(g,0,-.12,-.5,3,.24,1,mats.floor);
 for(let i=0;i<12;i++){
  const y=-(i+1)*.20,s=1+(i+.5)*.58;
  box(g,0,y-.12,-s,3,.24,.59,mats.floor);
  box(g,0,y+.012,-(1+i*.58),2.88,.025,.055,toolMats.tag);
 }
 box(g,0,-2.53,-9.06,3,.26,2.2,mats.floor);
 // Side walls and handrails follow the stair slope; no void is exposed.
 for(const side of [-1,1]){
  box(g,side*1.62,.1,-4.85,.24,7,9.7,mats.wall);
  for(let i=0;i<7;i++){
   const s=.55+i*1.38,y=-clamp((s-1)/6.96,0,1)*2.4;
   cylinder(g,side*1.39,y+.50,-s,.034,1,toolMats.steel);
  }
  limb(g,[side*1.39,1,-.5],[side*1.39,-1.4,-8.35],.036,.036,toolMats.steel,8);
 }
 box(g,0,3.3,-4.8,3.5,.22,10,mats.black);
 box(g,0,-.25,-10.1,3.5,4.5,.25,mats.wall);
 const gate=new It();g.add(gate);
 for(let i=-4;i<=4;i++)box(gate,i*.32,1.35,-.65,.055,2.7,.055,mats.metal);
 box(gate,0,1.32,-.65,2.9,.08,.09,mats.rust);
 sign(g,0,.25,-9.94,'AKSES / '+roomTitles[roomOrder.indexOf(next)].split(' — ')[1],2.6,.42);
 const l=light(room,room.cx,1.3,z-4.8,0xb5c5b1,55,14);
 factoryStairs[id]={id,next,g,z,gate,unlocked,passable,length:10};
}
addFactoryStairs('loading','relay',()=>state.puzzles.panel.solved,()=>state.flags.overseerDefeated&&state.flags.flashBreakComplete);
addFactoryStairs('relay','pursuit',()=>state.flags.relaySolved,()=>state.flags.relaySolved);
addFactoryStairs('production','office',()=>state.puzzles.valve.solved,()=>state.flags.handsDefeated);
addFactoryStairs('office','ritual',()=>state.puzzles.breaker.solved,()=>state.puzzles.breaker.solved);
addFactoryStairs('ritual','corridor',()=>state.flags.tumbalDefeated,()=>state.flags.tumbalDefeated);
function stairFloor(x,z){
 if(state.room==='pursuit')return pursuitFloor(x,z);
 const s=factoryStairs[state.room];
 if(!s||Math.abs(x-rooms[state.room].cx)>1.55||z>s.z-1)return 0;
 return -.2*Math.min(12,Math.max(0,Math.floor((s.z-z-1)/.58)+1));
}
function updateStairs(){
 const s=factoryStairs[state.room];if(!s)return;
 s.gate.visible=!s.passable();
 if(!sequence&&!overlay&&!fade&&!currentFight&&s.passable()&&player.z<s.z-9.15)enterRoom(s.next);
}

 if(!s.passable())toast('Palang terkunci');
}

// Door targets and lock ownership are independent. Picking up a key never
// changes a hinge target. Only interaction or an authored encounter opens it.
function moveDoor(d,dt){
 const m=d.motion;if(!m){d.state=d.open?'open':d.locked?'locked':'closed';return;}m.time=Math.min(m.duration,m.time+dt);const t=m.time/m.duration,e=t*t*(3-2*t);
 d.pivot.rotation.y=m.from+(m.to-m.from)*e;
 for(let i=0;i<d.handles.length;i++)d.handles[i].rotation.z=(i?1:-1)*Math.sin(Math.min(1,t*2.8)*Math.PI)*.28;
 if(t>=1){d.pivot.rotation.y=d.target;d.motion=null;d.state=d.open?'open':d.locked?'locked':'closed';for(const h of d.handles)h.rotation.z=0;}
}
function useAnnexDoor(a){
 const d=doors[a.doorKey];if(d.motion||d.actorReserved)return;
 if(!a.unlocked()){He.click(.15);return;}
 if(d.open&&Math.abs(player.x-a.wallX)<.8&&Math.abs(player.z-a.z)<1.5)return;
 const open=!d.open;d.locked=false;exploration().doors[a.id]=open;setDoorOpen(a.doorKey,open);saveProgress();
}
// The beam is still the original camera spotlight. Its gain is synchronized
// with raising the held model, with no extra light, listener or render loop.
const lampAnimation={amount:0,light:0,walk:0,phase:0,x:0,z:0,tool:null};
function resetLampPose(){lampAnimation.amount=lampAnimation.light=lampAnimation.walk=0;lampAnimation.x=player.x;lampAnimation.z=player.z;lampAnimation.tool=!!state.flags.lenteraOwned;}
function lampBeamReady(){return flashlight&&lampAvailable()&&lampAnimation.light>.45;}
function updateHeldToolPose(dt){
 const a=lampAnimation,wanted=flashlight&&lampAvailable()&&!lenteraTake;
 a.amount+=(Number(wanted)-a.amount)*(1-Math.exp(-dt*7));
 const lit=wanted?clamp((a.amount-.62)/.30,0,1):0;a.light+=(lit-a.light)*(1-Math.exp(-dt*(wanted?20:15)));
 const distance=Math.hypot(player.x-a.x,player.z-a.z);a.x=player.x;a.z=player.z;
 const speed=dt>0?Math.min(6,distance/dt):0;a.walk+=(Math.min(1,speed/4)-a.walk)*(1-Math.exp(-dt*8));a.phase+=dt*(2+speed*2.1);
 const bob=Math.sin(a.phase)*.008*a.walk,roll=Math.cos(a.phase*.5)*.012*a.walk,idle=Math.sin(now*1.65)*.0018;
 if(state.flags.lenteraOwned){
  // Lamp switch motion never holsters the independent mechanical claw.
  heldLentera.root.position.set(.32+Math.cos(a.phase*.5)*.004*a.walk,-.255+bob+idle-(1-a.amount)*.018,-.66+(1-a.amount)*.012);
  heldLentera.root.rotation.set(-(1-a.amount)*.035+Math.sin(a.phase)*.012*a.walk,Math.cos(a.phase*.5)*.01*a.walk,-.045+roll);
 }else{
  const ease=a.amount*a.amount*(3-2*a.amount);
  oldFlashVisual.position.set(.25+Math.cos(a.phase*.5)*.005*a.walk,-.245-(1-ease)*.30+bob+idle,-.49+(1-ease)*.08);
  oldFlashVisual.rotation.set(-(1-ease)*.62+Math.sin(a.phase)*.012*a.walk,0,roll);
 }
 const tension=toolReel?.phase==='hold'?1:0;a.tension=(a.tension||0)+(tension-(a.tension||0))*(1-Math.exp(-dt*10));
 heldLentera.root.rotation.x-=a.tension*.045;heldLentera.root.position.z+=a.tension*.016;
 const yawDelta=Math.atan2(Math.sin(player.yaw-(a.lastYaw??player.yaw)),Math.cos(player.yaw-(a.lastYaw??player.yaw))),pitchDelta=player.pitch-(a.lastPitch??player.pitch);a.lastYaw=player.yaw;a.lastPitch=player.pitch;a.swayYaw=(a.swayYaw||0)+(clamp(yawDelta/Math.max(dt,.001)*-.008,-.035,.035)-(a.swayYaw||0))*(1-Math.exp(-dt*9));a.swayPitch=(a.swayPitch||0)+(clamp(pitchDelta/Math.max(dt,.001)*-.008,-.025,.025)-(a.swayPitch||0))*(1-Math.exp(-dt*9));
 for(const model of [oldFlashVisual,heldLentera.root]){model.rotation.y+=a.swayYaw;model.rotation.x+=a.swayPitch;}
 toolMats.lens.color.set(a.light>.35&&beamCooldown<=0?0xc7c6a2:0x374339);
 if(oldFlashVisual.visible&&!state.flags.lenteraOwned){Wn.position.copy(oldFlashVisual.position);Wn.position.z-=.15;}else{Wn.position.copy(heldLentera.root.position);Wn.position.y+=.095;Wn.position.z-=.17;}
}
function turnBossTowardPlayer(dt){const q=Ee.quaternion.clone();Ee.lookAt(player.x,0,player.z);const next=Ee.quaternion.clone();Ee.quaternion.copy(q).slerp(next,1-Math.exp(-dt*7));}

const tutorialVideoURL="[EMBEDDED_ASSET:video/webm]",tutorialPosterURL="[EMBEDDED_ASSET:image/jpeg]";
const MechanicTutorial=(()=>{

const uiHints={queue:[],active:null,left:0,cooldown:0,shown:0,focusClock:0};
function uiBusy(){return paused||!!overlay||!!sequence||!!fade||!!daisyAnimation||!!daisyCredits||!!devour||!!daisyRescue||['title','ending'].includes(state.room);}
function learnedHints(){const f=state.flags;if(!f.uiLearned||typeof f.uiLearned!=='object')f.uiLearned=f.lenteraTutorialDone?{claw:true}:{};return f.uiLearned;}
function hintOnce(id,text,combat=false){if(learnedHints()[id]||uiHints.active?.id===id||uiHints.queue.some(h=>h.id===id))return;uiHints.queue.push({id,text,combat,room:state.room});}
function resetContextUI(){target=null;toolRayHint=null;$('prompt').style.opacity=0;$('prompt').textContent='';delete $('prompt').dataset.focus;$('subtitle').classList.remove('show');$('subtitle').textContent='';subtitleLeft=0;$('toast').classList.remove('show');toastLeft=0;tutorialNotice.classList.remove('show');uiHints.queue=[];uiHints.active=null;uiHints.left=0;uiHints.cooldown=.8;}
function updateImmersiveUI(dt){
 const busy=uiBusy(),cinema=!!sequence||!!daisyAnimation||!!daisyCredits||!!devour||!!daisyRescue;
 document.body.classList.toggle('kala-cinematic',cinema);
 document.body.classList.toggle('kala-ui-covered',busy);
 if(busy){$('prompt').style.opacity=0;tutorialNotice.classList.remove('show');return;}
 if(uiHints.active){uiHints.left-=dt;if(currentFight&&!uiHints.active.combat||state.room==='pursuit'){uiHints.left=0;}if(uiHints.left<=0){uiHints.active=null;tutorialNotice.classList.remove('show');uiHints.cooldown=1.5;}else tutorialNotice.classList.add('show');}
 else if(uiHints.cooldown>0)uiHints.cooldown-=dt;
 else {
  const safe=state.room!=='pursuit'&&(!currentFight||(!currentFight.windup&&!currentFight.dash&&currentFight.hitTimer>.4));
  uiHints.queue=uiHints.queue.filter(h=>h.room===state.room&&!learnedHints()[h.id]);
  const i=uiHints.queue.findIndex(h=>safe&&(!currentFight||h.combat));
  if(i>=0){const h=uiHints.queue.splice(i,1)[0];uiHints.active=h;uiHints.left=4.2;uiHints.shown++;tutorialNotice.textContent=h.text;tutorialNotice.classList.add('show');learnedHints()[h.id]=true;saveProgress();}
 }
 // Ray tests run at most 12.5 times per second. E still refreshes immediately.
 uiHints.focusClock-=dt;if(uiHints.focusClock<=0){uiHints.focusClock=.08;nearest();}
 else if(target){const x=target.npc?target.npc.position.x:target.x,z=target.npc?target.npc.position.z:target.z;if(!target.available()||Math.hypot(player.x-x,player.z-z)>target.radius){target=null;$('prompt').style.opacity=0;}}
}
function shortInteractionLabel(it){
 const s=it.label.trim();
 if(it.npc)return 'Bicara dengan '+(it.npc.userData.name||s.replace(/^bicara dengan /i,''));
 if(/Ruang BK/i.test(s))return 'Bicara dengan Bu Ratna';
 if(/loker Raka/i.test(s))return 'Periksa loker';
 if(/buka lemari kaca/i.test(s))return 'Buka lemari Daisy';
 if(/mural/i.test(s))return 'Amati gambar';
 if(/album dan perjanjian/i.test(s))return 'Ambil album';
 if(/sobekan foto|ambil dan periksa foto/i.test(s))return 'Ambil foto';
 if(s==='agenda Sari')return 'Ambil agenda Sari';
 if(/^kotak sekering/.test(s))return 'Periksa sekering';
 if(/^baca /i.test(s)){let title=s.slice(5).toLocaleLowerCase('id-ID').replace(/\b(danu|raka|sari|adit)\b/g,n=>n[0].toUpperCase()+n.slice(1)).replace(/lentera-g/gi,TOOL_NAME);return title.length>37?'Baca catatan':'Baca '+title;}
 if(/^ambil /i.test(s)){const name=s.slice(6);return 'Ambil '+(name===TOOL_NAME?name:name[0].toLowerCase()+name.slice(1));}
 if(/jalur servis/.test(s))return 'Periksa jalur servis';
 if(/panel|saklar|katup|pemutar|catu/i.test(s))return 'Periksa '+s.replace(/^periksa /i,'').replace(/ ·.*/,'');
 if(/pintu|Gudang Kartini|gerbang|jalur servis/i.test(s))return 'Buka pintu';
 return s.charAt(0).toUpperCase()+s.slice(1);
}
function interactionContext(it){
 if(it.annex){const a=it.annex,d=doors[a.doorKey];d.locked=!a.unlocked();if(!d.motion&&!d.open)d.state=d.locked?'locked':'closed';if(['opening','closing'].includes(d.state))return {text:d.state==='opening'?'Membuka…':'Menutup…',key:''};if(d.actorReserved)return {text:d.open?'Terbuka':'Terkunci',key:''};if(!a.unlocked())return {text:'Terkunci',key:''};return {text:d.open?'Tutup pintu':'Buka pintu',key:'E'};}
 const s=it.label,f=state.flags,p=state.puzzles;
 const locked=/terkunci dari luar/.test(s)||(/masuk Gudang Kartini/.test(s)&&!p.fuse.solved)||(/pintu utara/.test(s)&&(!p.panel.solved||!f.overseerDefeated))||(/pintu kantor/.test(s)&&(!p.valve.solved||!f.handsDefeated))||(/pintu ruang ritual/.test(s)&&!p.breaker.solved)||(/pintu lorong pemeliharaan/.test(s)&&!f.tumbalDefeated)||(/periksa pintu keluar/.test(s)&&f.exitLocked)||(/D–08/.test(s)&&!f.exitLocked);
 if(locked)return {text:'Terkunci',key:''};
 if(/pintu arsip/.test(s)&&!f.relaySolved||/gerbang selatan/.test(s)&&(!state.evidence.A||!f.invited)||/jalur servis/.test(s)&&!state.evidence.B)return {text:'Terkunci',key:''};
 if(/pintu|Gudang Kartini/.test(s)&&factoryStairs[state.room]?.passable())return {text:'Turuni tangga',key:'E'};
 return {text:shortInteractionLabel(it),key:'E'};
}
function showInteractionPrompt(key,text){const p=$('prompt'),signature=key+'|'+text;if(p.dataset.focus!==signature){p.dataset.focus=signature;p.replaceChildren();if(key){const k=document.createElement('kbd');k.textContent=key;p.append(k);}p.append(document.createTextNode(text));}p.style.opacity=1;}
function findInteraction(){
 if(uiBusy()||currentFight||lenteraTake)return null;
 const origin=ct.getWorldPosition(new I()),forward=ct.getWorldDirection(new I()),list=[];
 for(const it of interactions){if(it.room!==state.room||!it.available())continue;const x=it.npc?it.npc.position.x:it.x,z=it.npc?it.npc.position.z:it.z;
  const dist=Math.hypot(player.x-x,player.z-z);if(dist>it.radius)continue;
  const point=new I(x,it.npc?1.45:(it.focusY??it.markerBase??1.35),z),delta=point.clone().sub(origin),length=delta.length(),dot=length>.1?forward.dot(delta.clone().normalize()):1;
  if(dot<.70)continue;list.push({it,point,length,score:(1-dot)*2+dist/it.radius});
 }
 list.sort((a,b)=>a.score-b.score);
 for(const c of list.slice(0,3)){const delta=c.point.clone().sub(origin).normalize(),hit=castRoomSurface(origin,delta);if(!hit||hit.distance>=c.length-.48)return c.it;}
 return null;
}
function shortToolLabel(label){if(/^RELAI /.test(label))return 'Putar relai '+label.slice(6);const labels={'PENGUNCI ARSIP':'Tarik pengunci arsip','RODA KATUP II':'Ambil roda katup II','REM KIPAS':'Tarik rem kipas','KISI VENTILASI':'Buka kisi ventilasi','UNIT R-17':'Ambil unit R-17','SOKET R-17':'Pasang unit R-17','TUAS ARSIP':'Tarik tuas arsip','PANEL KATUP':'Periksa panel katup','PANEL SAKLAR':'Periksa panel saklar','PANEL KONTROL':'Periksa panel kontrol'};return labels[label]||label.replace(/^LILIN /,'Lilin ').replace(/ · (\d)\/3$/,'  $1/3').replace(/^JURU ABU · (\d+) HP$/,'Juru Abu · $1 HP').replace(/^TANGKAP TANGAN$/,'Tangkis tangan').replace(/^TANGKAP LENDIR$/,'Tangkis lendir').replace(/^LEPAS TANGAN$/,'Lepaskan tangan');}
function refreshContextPrompt(){
 target=null;if(uiBusy()){$('prompt').style.opacity=0;return;}
 target=findInteraction();if(target){const c=interactionContext(target);showInteractionPrompt(c.key,c.text);return;}
 if(toolReel?.phase==='hold'&&toolReel.hit.target.surface){showInteractionPrompt('R','Lepaskan tambatan');hintOnce('tether','Shift  Tarik tubuh');return;}
 if(toolReel){$('prompt').style.opacity=0;return;}
 const hit=toolRayHint;
 if(hit&&!hit.target.surface&&hit.distance<=22&&hit.target.available?.()!==false&&(!hit.target.bolt||bolts.includes(hit.target.bolt))&&(!hit.target.hand||!hit.target.hand.disabled)){showInteractionPrompt('R',shortToolLabel(hit.target.label));return;}
 $('prompt').style.opacity=0;
}

// Markers identify readable objects without replacing physical exploration.
function interact(room,x,z,label,fn,opts={}){const item={room,x,z,label,fn,radius:opts.radius||2.4,available:opts.available||(()=>true),marker:null,focusY:opts.y};if(opts.marker!==false){const g=new It();const p=new ae(new _t(.20,.28),new Xe({color:opts.color||0xded3b0,emissive:0x9b8854,emissiveIntensity:.35,side:xn}));g.add(p);g.userData.noGrip=true;g.position.set(x,opts.y||1.1,z);rooms[room].group.add(g);item.marker=g;item.markerBase=g.position.y;allMarkers.push(item)}interactions.push(item);return item}

function photoIsBack(){return Math.abs(Math.round(photoAngle/180))%2===1;}
function resetPhotoDrag(){if(photoDrag){const id=photoDrag.id;photoDrag=null;try{if($('photoCard').hasPointerCapture(id))$('photoCard').releasePointerCapture(id);}catch{}}$('photoCard').classList.remove('dragging');}

 const back=photoIsBack();$('photoFront').setAttribute('aria-hidden',String(back));$('photoBack').setAttribute('aria-hidden',String(!back));$('photoCard').setAttribute('aria-label',`Foto sisi ${back?'belakang':'depan'}. Geser untuk membalik.`);

}

function flipPhoto(){resetPhotoDrag();setPhotoAngle(photoAngle+180);}
$('photoClose').onclick=closeLayer;$('photoFlip').onclick=flipPhoto;
$('photoCard').addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{if(e.button!==0||photoDrag)return;e.preventDefault();photoSuppressClick=false;photoDrag={id:e.pointerId,start:e.clientX,base:photoAngle,dx:0};$('photoCard').classList.add('dragging');$('photoCard').setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);});
$('photoCard').addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(!photoDrag||e.pointerId!==photoDrag.id)return;photoDrag.dx=e.clientX-photoDrag.start;const angle=photoDrag.base+clamp(photoDrag.dx/$('photoCard').clientWidth*270,-180,180);$('photoCard').style.transform=`rotateY(${angle}deg) rotateZ(-1.2deg)`;});
$('photoCard').addEventListener('pointerup',e=>{if(!photoDrag||e.pointerId!==photoDrag.id)return;const {base,dx}=photoDrag;photoSuppressClick=Math.abs(dx)>6;resetPhotoDrag();setPhotoAngle(Math.abs(dx)>40?base+Math.sign(dx)*180:base);});
$('photoCard').addEventListener('pointercancel',()=>{if(!photoDrag)return;const base=photoDrag.base;photoSuppressClick=true;resetPhotoDrag();setPhotoAngle(base,false);});
$('photoCard').addEventListener('lostpointercapture',()=>{if(!photoDrag)return;const base=photoDrag.base;resetPhotoDrag();setPhotoAngle(base,false);});
$('photoCard').onclick=()=>{if(photoSuppressClick){photoSuppressClick=false;return;}flipPhoto();};
$('photoCard').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.code==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();flipPhoto();}});

function countEvidence(){return Object.values(state.evidence).filter(Boolean).length}
function collectEvidence(id){if(!state.evidence[id]){state.evidence[id]=true;state.sanity=clamp(state.sanity+5,0,100);toast('Bukti disimpan');}if(id==='op1'){normalisePhotoEvidence(state);openPhotoInspector();saveProgress();return;}const [t,b]=evidenceContent[id];openNote(t,b,'ev_'+id);saveProgress();}

// Students speak in the scene; their full conversations still belong to the journal.
const StudentChat=(()=>{
 let active=null;
 function spokenText(body){return body.replace(/\\n/g,'\n').replace(/\n\nKau menandai undangan Danu di jurnal\.$/,'').trim();}
 function status(speaking){$('studentDialogueStatus').classList.toggle('speaking',speaking);$('studentDialogueStatus').textContent=active?.voiceId?(speaking?'BERBICARA…':'DIALOG SELESAI'):'';}
 function replay(){if(!active?.voiceId||paused)return;status(true);const session=active;const played=Voice.play(active.voiceId,'conversation',()=>{if(active===session)status(false)});if(!played)status(false);}
 function open(name,body,id,{replay:archived=false}={}){
  if(!openLayer('studentDialogue'))return false;
  Voice.stop();Voice.clearNote();
  const npc=!archived&&state.room==='school'&&npcMap[name]?.visible?npcMap[name]:null;
  active={name,id,body,archived,voiceId:Voice.forNote(id),npc,oldYaw:npc?.rotation.y,oldHeadYaw:npc?.userData.head.rotation.y};
  if(npc){
   npc.rotation.y=Math.atan2(player.x-npc.position.x,player.z-npc.position.z);npc.userData.head.rotation.y=0;
   ct.lookAt(npc.position.x,npc.position.y+1.32*npc.scale.y,npc.position.z);player.yaw=ct.rotation.y;player.pitch=clamp(ct.rotation.x,-1.2,1.2);ct.rotation.set(player.pitch,player.yaw,0);ct.updateMatrixWorld(true);
  }
  $('studentDialogueName').textContent=name.toUpperCase();$('studentDialogueMode').textContent=archived?'ARSIP PERCAKAPAN':'PERCAKAPAN';
  $('studentDialogueText').textContent=spokenText(body);$('studentDialogueText').scrollTop=0;
  $('studentDialogueReplay').hidden=!active.voiceId;document.body.classList.add('student-chat-open');
  if(id&&!state.seen[id]){state.seen[id]=true;state.notes.push({id,title:name.toUpperCase(),body});saveProgress();}
  status(false);if(active.voiceId)replay();$('studentDialogueClose').focus({preventScroll:true});updateHUD();return true;
 }
 function close(){
  Voice.stop();if(active?.npc){active.npc.rotation.y=active.oldYaw;active.npc.userData.head.rotation.y=active.oldHeadYaw;}
  active=null;document.body.classList.remove('student-chat-open');$('studentDialogueStatus').classList.remove('speaking');
 }
 function stateInfo(){
  const npc=active?.npc,point=npc?new I(npc.position.x,npc.position.y+1.56*npc.scale.y,npc.position.z).project(ct):null;
  return active?{name:active.name,id:active.id,archived:active.archived,voiceId:active.voiceId,actorVisible:!!npc?.visible,headScreen:point?{x:(point.x+1)*innerWidth/2,y:(1-point.y)*innerHeight/2,z:point.z}:null}:null;
 }
 $('studentDialogueClose').onclick=closeLayer;$('studentDialogueReplay').onclick=replay;
 return {open,close,replay,state:stateInfo};
})();
function openStudentDialogue(name,body,id,options){return StudentChat.open(name,body,id,options);}

function schoolDialogueLines(after){return {
 Sari:['SARI','Aku pengin Danu aman. Cuma… timeline ceritanya ada yang nggak cocok. Agendaku ketinggalan di bangku warung Jalan Kartini. Coba cek, deh.\n\nRaka sempat nulis dua angka di belakang foto yang dibawa Adit. Dua angka terakhir dia bisikin ke aku: 2 sama 9.\n\nCari suratnya dulu, ya. Jangan buru-buru nuduh siapa-siapa.'],
 Adit:['ADIT','Lokernya di sebelah barat, dekat lab. Sebelum pulang, Raka ngecek terus. Kayak takut ada yang ngambil isinya.\n\nOh, fotonya kutaruh di kelas 1A. Sayap barat, lewat koridor tengah. Cek bangku paling belakang.\n\nAda yang off, sih. Tiap kejadian, Danu selalu tahu posisi aku sama Sari. Kebetulan… mungkin?'],
 Raka:['RAKA',after?'Kamu udah baca suratnya? Jangan kasih lihat Danu. Please.\n\nAku cuma bilang kita harus berhenti. Semua orang dengar aku ngomong… tapi nggak ada yang dengar dia jawab apa.\n\nKalau kamu tetap ke gudang, baca semua memo. Catatan petugas urutannya shift, truk, jam datang, terus gudang. Itu aja yang bisa kubilang sekarang.':'Aku cuma nggak mau ada yang kena lagi. Serius.\n\nIya, kamu lihat aku di depan Danu. Tapi kamu nggak lihat yang terjadi sebelum itu.\n\nKalau mau cari tahu, dengerin semuanya dulu. Jangan langsung mutusin siapa yang salah.'],
 Danu:['DANU',after?'Jadi… surat itu di kamu? Oke, aku bisa jelasin.\n\nHabis bel, lewat Jalan Kartini. Ada gudang tua di ujung gang. Datang sendiri, ya. Kalau rame-rame, orang malah ngerusak ceritanya.\n\n…Buktinya. Maksudku, ngerusak bukti.\n\nKau menandai undangan Danu di jurnal.':'Tadi kamu lihat Raka, kan? Ya… aku udah biasa, kok.\n\nNggak usah terlalu banyak tanya Sari. Dia suka overthinking, terus nyatet yang nggak-nggak.\n\n…Thanks, ya. Udah peduli.' ]};}
function migrateSchoolDialogues(s){for(const note of s.notes){const m=/^talk_(Danu|Raka|Sari|Adit)_(before|after)$/.exec(note.id);if(m)note.body=schoolDialogueLines(m[2]==='after')[m[1]][1];}s.flags.schoolDialogueRevision=2;}
function npcDialogue(name){setNpcExpression(name,name==='Danu'?(state.evidence.A?'happy':'sad'):name==='Adit'?'laugh':'sad',name==='Adit'?2:0);const conversations=schoolDialogueLines(!!state.evidence.A);
 const [t,b]=conversations[name];if(name==='Sari')state.flags.lockerHint=true;if(name==='Danu'&&state.evidence.A)state.flags.invited=true;openStudentDialogue(name,b,'talk_'+name+'_'+(state.evidence.A?'after':'before'));saveProgress();
}

function toast(text){if(!text||$('subtitle').classList.contains('show'))return;if(uiBusy()||state.room==='pursuit')return;if($('toast').textContent===text&&toastLeft>0)return;$('toast').textContent=text;$('toast').classList.add('show');toastLeft=2.1;}
function subtitle(text,duration=5){if(!text)return;$('subtitle').textContent=text;$('subtitle').classList.add('show');const voiceId=Voice.forText(text),spoken=voiceId?Voice.play(voiceId,sequence?'sequence':'subtitle'):0;subtitleLeft=Math.max(duration,spoken+.35)}
function clearKeys(){keys={};drag=false}
let pointerLockEpoch=0,pointerLockPending=false,pointerDeltaFresh=true;
function unlock(){pointerLockEpoch++;clearKeys();if(document.pointerLockElement){expectedUnlock=true;document.exitPointerLock?.()}}
function lock(){if(daisyCredits||paused||overlay||fade||state.room==='ending'||state.room==='title'||document.pointerLockElement===lt.domElement||pointerLockPending)return;
 const epoch=++pointerLockEpoch,fallback=()=>{pointerLockPending=false;if(epoch===pointerLockEpoch&&!paused&&!overlay)hintOnce('mouse_drag','Seret mouse untuk melihat');};
 try{pointerLockPending=true;const p=lt.domElement.requestPointerLock?.();if(p?.then)p.then(()=>{pointerLockPending=false;if(epoch!==pointerLockEpoch||paused||overlay||fade||['title','ending'].includes(state.room)){if(document.pointerLockElement===lt.domElement){expectedUnlock=true;document.exitPointerLock?.();}}}).catch(fallback);else pointerLockPending=false;}catch{fallback();}
}
function openLayer(id){if(overlay)return false;tutorialNotice.classList.remove('show');cancelToolReach();overlay=id;clearKeys();unlock();target=null;$('prompt').style.opacity=0;$(id).classList.add('show');return true}
function closeLayer(relock=true){if(!overlay)return;if(overlay==='puzzle'&&puzzleResultTime>0)return;if(overlay==='mechanicTutorial'){MechanicTutorial.close(true,relock);return;}if(overlay==='studentDialogue')StudentChat.close();if(overlay==='doc'){Voice.stop();Voice.clearNote();}if(overlay==='photoInspector')resetPhotoDrag();$(overlay).classList.remove('show');if(overlay==='puzzle'){puzzleOpen=null}overlay=null;clearKeys();updateHUD();if(relock!==false)lock()}
function openNote(title,body,id){const student=/^talk_(Danu|Raka|Sari|Adit)_(before|after)$/.exec(id||'');if(student)return openStudentDialogue(student[1],body,id,{replay:true});if(!openLayer('doc'))return;$('docTitle').textContent=title;$('docBody').textContent=body;Voice.note(id);if(id&&!state.seen[id]){state.seen[id]=true;state.notes.push({id,title,body});saveProgress()}if(id==='ev_B'&&!state.flags.reveal){state.flags.reveal=true;state.sanity=clamp(state.sanity+5,0,100);saveProgress()}updateHUD()}
$('docCloseBtn').onclick=closeLayer;$('doc').onclick=e=>{if(e.target===$('doc'))closeLayer()};
function openChoice(title,body,choices){if(!openLayer('choicePanel'))return;$('choicePanel').innerHTML='';const h=document.createElement('h3');h.textContent=title;$('choicePanel').append(h);const p=document.createElement('p');p.className='sit';p.textContent=body;$('choicePanel').append(p);choices.forEach((c,i)=>{const b=document.createElement('button');b.className='opt';b.textContent=`${i+1}. ${c.text}`;b.disabled=!!c.disabled;b.onclick=()=>{if(paused)return;closeLayer();c.action()};$('choicePanel').append(b)})}
function openJournal(){if(overlay){if(overlay==='journal')closeLayer();return}if(!openLayer('journal'))return;$('journalChapter').textContent=roomTitles[roomOrder.indexOf(state.room)]||'';$('journalObjective').textContent=objective();$('journalExploration').textContent=Object.keys(exploration().visited).length+'/8 ruang samping dijelajahi';$('journalEvidence').textContent=`${countEvidence()}/4 bukti utama · ${state.notes.length} catatan`;inventoryJournal();const list=$('journalList');list.innerHTML='';if(!state.notes.length){list.textContent='Dokumen dan percakapan yang telah dibaca akan tersimpan di sini.';return}for(const n of state.notes){const d=document.createElement('details');const s=document.createElement('summary');s.textContent=n.title;const p=document.createElement('p');p.textContent=n.body;d.append(s,p);if(/^talk_(Danu|Raka|Sari|Adit)_(before|after)$/.test(n.id)){const b=document.createElement('button');b.className='journalPhotoButton';b.textContent='BUKA PERCAKAPAN';b.onclick=()=>{closeLayer(false);openNote(n.title,n.body,n.id)};d.append(b);}if(n.id==='ev_op1'){const b=document.createElement('button');b.className='journalPhotoButton';b.textContent='PERIKSA FOTO';b.onclick=()=>{closeLayer();openPhotoInspector()};d.append(b);}list.append(d)}}

// Side rooms belong to their existing chapter. Saves keep the same room IDs.
const annexes=[],explorationItems={},explorationProps=[];
const explorationItemNames={maintenanceKey:['Kunci suku cadang','Kunci kecil dengan label inventaris gudang.'],panelFuse:['Sekering operator','Sekering keramik untuk catu panel bangsal muat.'],generatorContact:['Kontak generator','Kontak tembaga yang masih utuh.'],workshopKey:['Kunci bengkel','Kunci yang disimpan petugas perawatan pekerja.'],valveWheel:['Roda katup II','Roda penggerak tekanan dengan poros segi empat.'],tapeReel:['Pita audit','Pita magnetik bertanggal 17 Maret.']};
function normaliseExploration(s){
 const old=!s.exploration||s.exploration.version!==1;
 if(old)s.exploration={version:1,inventory:{},installed:{},visited:{},gates:{}};
 const e=s.exploration;for(const k of ['inventory','installed','visited','gates','doors'])if(!e[k]||typeof e[k]!=='object'||Array.isArray(e[k]))e[k]={};
 if(old){
  if(s.puzzles.panel.solved||s.flags.overseerDefeated){e.installed.panelFuse=true;e.inventory.maintenanceKey=true;}
  if(s.flags.relaySolved||['production','office','ritual','corridor','daisy'].includes(s.room)){e.installed.generatorContact=true;e.gates.relayArchive=true;}
  if(s.puzzles.valve.solved||s.flags.handsDefeated){e.installed.valveWheel=true;e.inventory.workshopKey=true;}
  if(s.puzzles.breaker.solved||['ritual','corridor','daisy'].includes(s.room)){e.installed.tapeReel=true;e.auditVerified=true;}
 }
 return e;
}
function exploration(){return normaliseExploration(state);}
function ownsItem(id){const e=exploration();return !!e.inventory[id]||!!e.installed[id];}
function giveItem(id){
 const e=exploration();if(ownsItem(id))return;e.inventory[id]=true;
 He.click(.25);toast(explorationItemNames[id][0]);saveProgress();lastHudSignature='';
}
function installItem(id){const e=exploration();if(e.installed[id])return true;if(!e.inventory[id])return false;delete e.inventory[id];e.installed[id]=true;He.click(.35);saveProgress();return true;}
function addAnnex(roomId,id,name,side,z,width,depth,unlocked=()=>true){
 const room=rooms[roomId],wallX=room.cx+side*room.width/2,cx=wallX+side*width/2,g=new It();g.name=id;room.group.add(g);
 const a={id,name,room:roomId,side,z,width,depth,cx,wallX,g,unlocked,minX:cx-width/2,maxX:cx+width/2,minZ:z-depth/2,maxZ:z+depth/2,lights:[]};annexes.push(a);
 box(g,cx,-.15,z,width,.3,depth,mats.floor);box(g,cx,3.65,z,width,.25,depth,mats.black);
 box(g,wallX+side*width,1.8,z,.24,3.6,depth,mats.wall);
 for(const zz of [a.minZ,a.maxZ])box(g,cx,1.8,zz,width,3.6,.24,mats.wall);
 // The rest of the former wall is rebuilt after all openings have been declared.
 const d=hingedDoor(room.group,wallX,z-1.32,2.64,2.95,0x45554b,-Math.PI/2,side<0?1.54:-1.54);a.doorKey='annex_'+id;doors[a.doorKey]=d;
 sign(room.group,wallX-side*.19,3.2,z,name,2.85,.32,side<0?Math.PI/2:-Math.PI/2);
 sign(g,wallX+side*(width-.18),2.55,z,name,Math.min(4,width-1),.35,side<0?Math.PI/2:-Math.PI/2);
 const before=room.group.children.length;const l=light(room,cx,3.1,z,0xc8bd9f,32,12);for(const child of room.group.children.slice(before))g.add(child);a.lights.push(l);
 const focus=interact(roomId,wallX,z,name,()=>{if(!d.actorReserved)useAnnexDoor(a);},{marker:false,radius:2.25,y:1.4});focus.annex=a;
 return a;
}

const archiveAnnex=addAnnex('relay','relay_archive','ARSIP RELAI',-1,-2,10,12,()=>!!exploration().gates.relayArchive);
const generatorAnnex=addAnnex('relay','generator','GENERATOR',1,8,12,12);

const infirmaryAnnex=addAnnex('production','infirmary','PERAWATAN PEKERJA',1,5,12,12);

const monitorAnnex=addAnnex('office','monitor','PEMANTAUAN',1,5.5,12,8);
function rebuildAnnexWalls(){
 for(const roomId of ['loading','relay','production','office']){
  const r=rooms[roomId];for(const side of [-1,1]){
   const x=r.cx+side*r.width/2,openings=annexes.filter(a=>a.room===roomId&&a.side===side).sort((a,b)=>a.z-b.z);
   for(const post of r.wallSupports||[])if(post.side===side){post.mesh.position.z=post.z;for(const a of openings)if(Math.abs(post.mesh.position.z-a.z)<1.88)post.mesh.position.z=a.z+(post.z<a.z?-1:1)*1.88;}
   for(const m of [...r.group.children]){const p=m.geometry?.parameters;if(m.isMesh&&p&&Math.abs(m.position.x-x)<.01&&p.width===.3&&p.depth===r.depth){r.group.remove(m);m.geometry.dispose();}}
   let cursor=-r.depth/2;for(const a of openings){const low=a.z-1.49,high=a.z+1.49;if(low>cursor)box(r.group,x,r.height/2,(cursor+low)/2,.3,r.height,low-cursor,mats.wall);box(r.group,x,(r.height+3.06)/2,a.z,.3,r.height-3.06,2.98,mats.wall);cursor=high;}
   if(cursor<r.depth/2)box(r.group,x,r.height/2,(cursor+r.depth/2)/2,.3,r.height,r.depth/2-cursor,mats.wall);
  }
 }
}
rebuildAnnexWalls();
function syncExplorationDoors(snap=false){const e=exploration();for(const a of annexes){const d=doors[a.doorKey];d.locked=!a.unlocked();if(!d.actorReserved)setDoorOpen(a.doorKey,!!e.doors[a.id]&&a.unlocked(),snap);if(!d.motion&&!d.open)d.state=d.locked?'locked':'closed';}}
function annexAt(x,z,roomId=state.room){return annexes.find(a=>a.room===roomId&&x>a.minX+.12&&x<a.maxX-.12&&z>a.minZ+.12&&z<a.maxZ-.12)||null;}
function annexSpace(x,z,radius){
 for(const a of annexes){if(a.room!==state.room||!a.unlocked())continue;
  const inside=x>a.minX+.18+radius&&x<a.maxX-.18-radius&&z>a.minZ+.18+radius&&z<a.maxZ-.18-radius;
  const neck=Math.abs(x-a.wallX)<1.25&&Math.abs(z-a.z)<1.27-radius;
  if(inside||neck&&Math.abs(doors[a.doorKey].pivot.rotation.y)>1.03)return a;
 }return null;
}
function annexDesk(a,x,z,w=2.4,d=1){
 box(a.g,x,.80,z,w,.16,d,mats.wood,true,rooms[a.room]);for(const side of [-1,1])for(const end of [-1,1])box(a.g,x+side*(w/2-.13),.36,z+end*(d/2-.10),.07,.72,.07,mats.metal);
}
function annexShelf(a,x,z,w=1.5,h=2.5,d=.6){
 const r=rooms[a.room];r.colliders.push({minX:x-w/2,maxX:x+w/2,minZ:z-d/2,maxZ:z+d/2});
 box(a.g,x,h/2,z-d/2+.035,w,h,.07,mats.metal);for(const side of [-1,1])box(a.g,x+side*(w/2-.035),h/2,z,.07,h,d,mats.metal);
 for(let i=0;i<4;i++){const shelfY=.20+i*.57;box(a.g,x,shelfY,z,w,.07,d,mats.rust);for(let j=0;j<3;j++)box(a.g,x-w/2+.27+j*.42,shelfY+.205,z+.025,.28,.34,d-.12,j%2?mats.paper:mats.wood);}
}
const annexMounts=[];
function annexWallBoard(a,id,x,y,w,h,side){
 const z=(side<0?a.minZ:a.maxZ)-side*.21,depth=.16;
 const board=box(a.g,x,y,z,w,h,depth,mats.wood);board.name=id;
 for(const sx of [-1,1])for(const sy of [-1,1]){
  const xx=x+sx*(w/2-.10),yy=y+sy*(h/2-.10);
  box(a.g,xx,yy,z+side*.095,.075,.075,.07,mats.metal);
  const bolt=cylinder(a.g,xx,yy,z-side*.092,.025,.028,toolMats.steel);bolt.rotation.x=Math.PI/2;
 }
 annexMounts.push({id,room:a.room,board,wallZ:side<0?a.minZ+.12:a.maxZ-.12,side});return z;
}
function archiveNote(a,x,z,title,body,id,y=.94){box(a.g,x,y-.025,z,.42,.035,.30,mats.paper);noteObject(a.room,x,z,title,body,id,{y,radius:1.85});}
function itemModel(a,id,x,y,z,kind){
 const g=new It();g.name='Item_'+id;g.position.set(x,y,z);a.g.add(g);
 if(kind==='key'){cylinder(g,0,0,0,.075,.025,toolMats.tag).rotation.x=Math.PI/2;box(g,0,-.13,0,.04,.22,.03,toolMats.steel);box(g,.035,-.21,0,.10,.045,.035,toolMats.steel);}
 if(kind==='fuse'){cylinder(g,0,0,0,.07,.30,mats.paper).rotation.z=Math.PI/2;for(const side of [-1,1])cylinder(g,side*.14,0,0,.075,.07,toolMats.steel).rotation.z=Math.PI/2;}
 if(kind==='contact'){box(g,0,0,0,.35,.04,.18,toolMats.rust);for(const side of [-1,1])cylinder(g,side*.13,.07,0,.033,.14,toolMats.tag);}
 if(kind==='wheel'){const disk=cylinder(g,0,0,0,.37,.07,mats.rust);disk.rotation.x=Math.PI/2;const face=cylinder(g,0,0,.05,.27,.08,toolMats.dark);face.rotation.x=Math.PI/2;for(let i=0;i<4;i++){const p=box(g,0,0,.10,.06,.70,.065,toolMats.steel);p.rotation.z=i*Math.PI/4;}box(g,0,0,.15,.12,.12,.12,toolMats.steel);}
 if(kind==='tape'){for(const side of [-1,1]){const reel=cylinder(g,side*.16,0,0,.16,.04,toolMats.steel);reel.rotation.x=Math.PI/2;const hub=cylinder(g,side*.16,0,.04,.05,.05,toolMats.dark);hub.rotation.x=Math.PI/2;}box(g,0,-.06,-.015,.36,.04,.04,toolMats.rust);}
 batchDollPieces(g);g.userData.noGrip=true;explorationItems[id]=g;explorationProps.push(g);return g;
}
function collectable(a,id,x,y,z,kind,grip=false){
 const g=itemModel(a,id,x,y,z,kind);
 if(grip)registerToolTarget(a.room,()=>g.getWorldPosition(new I()),.48,explorationItemNames[id][0].toUpperCase(),()=>giveItem(id),()=>!ownsItem(id));
 else interact(a.room,x,z,'ambil '+explorationItemNames[id][0],()=>giveItem(id),{y,radius:1.85,available:()=>!ownsItem(id)});
 return g;
}

function prepareExplorationPuzzle(id){
 const e=exploration();
 if(id==='panel'&&!e.installed.panelFuse){if(!installItem('panelFuse')){subtitle('Sekering belum dipasang.',4);return false;}toast('Sekering operator terpasang.');}
 if(id==='valve'&&!e.installed.valveWheel){if(!installItem('valveWheel')){subtitle('Roda katup II belum dipasang.',4);return false;}toast('Roda katup II terpasang pada poros.');}
 if(id==='breaker'&&!e.auditVerified){subtitle('Otorisasi audit belum terbaca.',4);return false;}
 return true;
}

}

let puzzleResultTime=0,puzzleHintShown=false;
function openPuzzle(id){if(state.puzzles[id].solved){subtitle('Panel sudah aktif.',3);return}if(!prepareExplorationPuzzle(id))return;if(!openLayer('puzzle'))return;puzzleOpen=id;puzzleResultTime=0;puzzleHintShown=false;$('puzzleHint').textContent='';$('puzzleStatus').textContent='';renderPuzzle();$('puzzleClose').focus()}
function renderPuzzle(){if(!puzzleOpen)return;const id=puzzleOpen,d=puzzleDefinitions[id],p=state.puzzles[id];$('puzzleTitle').textContent=d.title;$('puzzleDetail').textContent=d.detail;const content=$('puzzleContent');content.innerHTML='';
 if(d.kind==='keypad'){const display=document.createElement('div');display.className='keyDisplay';display.setAttribute('aria-live','polite');display.textContent=(p.entered+'····').slice(0,4);content.append(display);const grid=document.createElement('div');grid.className='keyGrid';for(const v of ['1','2','3','4','5','6','7','8','9','C','0','↵']){const b=document.createElement('button');b.textContent=v;b.onclick=()=>puzzleKey(v);grid.append(b)}content.append(grid)}
 if(d.kind==='valve'){const grid=document.createElement('div');grid.className='valveGrid';p.pos.forEach((pos,i)=>{const b=document.createElement('button');b.className='valveButton';b.setAttribute('aria-label',`Katup ${i+1}, posisi ${pos*90} derajat, putar 90 derajat`);b.innerHTML=`<span class="valveArrow" style="transform:rotate(${pos*90}deg)">↑</span><span>KATUP ${['I','II','III'][i]}</span>`;b.onclick=()=>{if(puzzleResultTime||paused)return;p.pos[i]=(p.pos[i]+1)%4;He.click(.18);renderPuzzle();if(p.pos.every(x=>x===0))solvePuzzle()};grid.append(b)});content.append(grid)}
 if(d.kind==='order'){const grid=document.createElement('div');grid.className='orderGrid';const labels=id==='fuse'?['M · MERAH','K · KUNING','B · BIRU']:['1','2','3','4'];labels.forEach((label,i)=>{const b=document.createElement('button');b.textContent=label;b.className='orderButton'+(p.current.includes(i)?' active':'');if(id==='fuse')b.style.setProperty('--wire',['#a84c48','#d5b95c','#507fad'][i]);b.onclick=()=>puzzleOrder(i);grid.append(b)});content.append(grid);const progress=document.createElement('p');progress.className='puzzleProgress';progress.textContent=`${p.current.length}/${d.order.length} tersambung`;content.append(progress)}
 $('puzzleContent').querySelectorAll('button').forEach(b=>b.disabled=p.solved);
}
function puzzleKey(v){if(!puzzleOpen||puzzleResultTime||paused)return;const p=state.puzzles[puzzleOpen],d=puzzleDefinitions[puzzleOpen];if(d.kind!=='keypad')return;if(v==='C')p.entered='';else if(v==='Backspace')p.entered=p.entered.slice(0,-1);else if(v==='↵'||v==='Enter'){if(p.entered===d.code){solvePuzzle();return}else{$('puzzleStatus').textContent='Kode belum cocok. Periksa petunjuk lalu coba lagi.';He.click(.35);p.entered=''}}else if(/^\d$/.test(v)&&p.entered.length<4)p.entered+=v;He.click(.08);renderPuzzle()}
function puzzleOrder(i){if(!puzzleOpen||puzzleResultTime||paused)return;const p=state.puzzles[puzzleOpen],d=puzzleDefinitions[puzzleOpen];if(i===d.order[p.current.length]){p.current.push(i);He.click(.15);$('puzzleStatus').textContent='Sambungan diterima.';if(p.current.length===d.order.length){renderPuzzle();solvePuzzle();return}}else{p.current=[];He.slam();$('puzzleStatus').textContent='Urutan terputus. Rangkaian diulang dari awal.'}renderPuzzle()}
function solvePuzzle(){const id=puzzleOpen;if(!id||state.puzzles[id].solved)return;state.puzzles[id].solved=true;puzzleResultTime=.9;He.click(.4);$('puzzleStatus').textContent='BERHASIL · kunci dilepas';$('puzzleStatus').classList.add('success');$('puzzleContent').querySelectorAll('button').forEach(b=>b.disabled=true);saveProgress();}
let officeExitActor=null;
function beginOfficeActor(){resetDanuRunPose();pt.visible=true;pt.position.set(803,0,-7);pt.rotation.set(0,0,0);setNpcExpression('Danu','laugh');officeExitActor={phase:'watch',node:0,gait:0,path:[new I(800,0,-8.5),new I(800,0,-15.5),new I(800,0,-19.15)]};}
function retreatOfficeActor(){if(officeExitActor)officeExitActor.phase='walk';}
function actorOccluded(root,heights=[.4,1,1.5]){const origin=ct.getWorldPosition(new I());for(const y of heights){const delta=root.position.clone().add(new I(0,y,0)).sub(origin),len=delta.length(),hit=castRoomSurface(origin,delta.normalize());if(!hit||hit.distance>len-.20)return false;}return true;}
function updateOfficeActor(dt){
 const a=officeExitActor;if(!a||state.room!=='office'||overlay||fade||paused)return;
 if(a.phase==='watch'){pt.userData.head.rotation.x=0;trackHead(pt.userData.head,pt,dt,.8);return;}
 const before=pt.position.clone(),goal=a.path[a.node];if(goal){const dx=goal.x-pt.position.x,dz=goal.z-pt.position.z,len=Math.hypot(dx,dz);if(len<.08)a.node++;else{const step=Math.min(len,3.8*dt);sweptEntityMove(pt,dx/len*step,dz/len*step,.24);const desired=Math.atan2(dx,dz);pt.rotation.y+=Math.atan2(Math.sin(desired-pt.rotation.y),Math.cos(desired-pt.rotation.y))*(1-Math.exp(-dt*8));}}
 pt.position.y=stairFloor(pt.position.x,pt.position.z);const distance=Math.hypot(pt.position.x-before.x,pt.position.z-before.z),moving=distance>dt*.15;a.gait+=distance*7;
 for(let i=0;i<2;i++){const stride=Math.sin(a.gait+i*Math.PI);pt.userData.runRig.legs[i].rotation.x=stride*(moving?.45:0);pt.userData.runRig.arms[i].rotation.x=-stride*(moving?.48:0)-(moving?.1:0);}pt.rotation.x=moving?-.06:0;
 if(!goal&&actorOccluded(pt)){pt.visible=false;officeExitActor=null;state.flags.danuOfficeDeparted=true;saveProgress();}
}
function creatureFootfall(root,strength=.018){
 if(!He.ctx||!He.master||paused||overlay||sequence||devour||['title','ending'].includes(state.room))return;const distance=Math.hypot(player.x-root.position.x,player.z-root.position.z);if(distance>12)return;
 const ctx=He.ctx,t=ctx.currentTime,gain=ctx.createGain(),tone=ctx.createOscillator(),pan=ctx.createStereoPanner();tone.type='triangle';tone.frequency.setValueAtTime(61,t);tone.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(31,t+.10);gain.gain.setValueAtTime(strength*Math.pow(1-distance/13,1.4),t);gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,t+.13);const dx=root.position.x-player.x,dz=root.position.z-player.z;pan.pan.value=clamp((dx*Math.cos(player.yaw)-dz*Math.sin(player.yaw))/Math.max(1,distance),-.8,.8);tone.connect(gain);gain.connect(pan);pan.connect(He.master);tone.onended=()=>{tone.disconnect();gain.disconnect();pan.disconnect();};tone.start(t);tone.stop(t+.14);
}
function syncCreatureStep(root,memory,gait,speed){const step=Math.floor(gait/Math.PI);if(memory.footstep!==undefined&&step!==memory.footstep&&speed>.6&&now>(memory.nextFootstep||0)){memory.nextFootstep=now+.19;creatureFootfall(root);}memory.footstep=step;}

Ia tidak lagi berpura-pura menangis.',duration:4},{text:'DANU: “Kau membaca terlalu jauh. Setiap cerita perlu seseorang yang disalahkan.”',duration:5},{text:'Ia menghilang ke ruang ritual. Pintu di depannya terbuka.',duration:3,action:retreatOfficeActor}],()=>{retreatOfficeActor();setDoorOpen('office',true);saveProgress()})}updateHUD()}

function runSequence(steps,onEnd,{cinematic=false}={}){clearKeys();target=null;$('prompt').style.opacity=0;tutorialNotice.classList.remove('show');$('toast').classList.remove('show');document.body.classList.add('kala-cinematic');sequence={steps,index:0,time:0,onEnd,cinematic,yaw:ct.rotation.y,pitch:ct.rotation.x};showSequenceStep();lock()}
function showSequenceStep(){if(!sequence)return;Voice.stop();const s=sequence.steps[sequence.index],voiceId=Voice.forText(s.text);sequence.stepDuration=Math.max(s.duration,voiceId?Voice.duration(voiceId)+.35:0);s.action?.();subtitle(s.text,sequence.stepDuration+1);sequence.stepVoiceClock=voiceId?Voice.clock():null;$('cinematicHint').textContent=sequence.cinematic?'E  Lewati':'E  Lanjut';$('cinematicHint').style.opacity=1;}
function nextSequence(skip=false){if(!sequence)return;if(skip){finishSequence();return}sequence.index++;sequence.time=0;if(sequence.index>=sequence.steps.length)finishSequence();else showSequenceStep()}
function finishSequence(){if(!sequence)return;Voice.stop();const cb=sequence.onEnd;player.yaw=sequence.yaw;player.pitch=sequence.pitch;sequence=null;clearKeys();$('cinematicHint').style.opacity=0;$('subtitle').classList.remove('show');cb?.();lock()}

let danuChase=null;
function resetDanuRunPose(){
 for(const joint of [...pt.userData.runRig.arms,...pt.userData.runRig.legs])joint.rotation.x=0;
 pt.userData.head.rotation.set(0,0,0);pt.rotation.x=pt.rotation.z=0;pt.position.y=0;
}
function cancelDanuChase(){if(danuChase){pt.visible=false;danuChase=null}resetDanuRunPose()}
function startDanuChase(){
 if(state.room!=='corridor'||state.flags.exitLocked||danuChase)return;
 cancelToolReach();clearKeys();target=null;$('prompt').style.opacity=0;
 player={x:1200,z:17,yaw:0,pitch:-.035};ct.position.set(player.x,1.62,player.z);ct.rotation.set(player.pitch,0,0);
 resetDanuRunPose();pt.visible=true;pt.position.set(1200.12,0,11.5);pt.rotation.y=Math.PI;setNpcExpression('Danu','sad');
 setDoorOpen('corridor',true,true);flashlight=true;
 danuChase={t:0,step:0,closing:false,slammed:false,latched:false};
 $('chapterCard').classList.remove('show');chapterLeft=0;
 runSequence([
  {text:'Danu menerobos lorong belakang. Kau berlari mengejarnya.',duration:2.3},
  {text:'AKU: “Danu! Berhenti!”',duration:3.0},
  {text:'Ia menoleh sekali, lalu mempercepat langkah menuju pintu keluar.',duration:2.9},
  {text:'Danu menyelinap melewati pintu. Kau masih beberapa langkah di belakangnya.',duration:1.6},
  {text:'DANU, dari luar: “Sampai di sini saja.” Kunci diputar. Langkahnya menjauh.',duration:3.2}
 ],finishDanuChase,{cinematic:true});
 updateDanuChase(0);updateHUD();
}
function updateDanuChase(dt){
 const a=danuChase;if(!a)return;a.t+=dt;
 const run=clamp(a.t/8.5,0,1),stride=a.t*16,stop=1-clamp((a.t-8.1)/.6,0,1);
 player.x=1200+Math.sin(a.t*1.3)*.11*stop;player.z=17-34.55*run;
 player.yaw=sequence.yaw;player.pitch=sequence.pitch;
 ct.position.set(player.x,1.62+Math.sin(stride)*.027*stop,player.z);
 const progress=clamp(a.t/7.8,0,1);
 pt.position.set(1200.12+Math.sin(a.t*1.7)*.14,.035*Math.abs(Math.sin(stride)),11.5-32.5*progress);
 pt.rotation.set(-.13,Math.PI+Math.sin(a.t*1.7)*.035,Math.sin(stride)*.018);
 pt.userData.head.rotation.y=a.t>4.7&&a.t<5.7?.64:0;
 for(let i=0;i<2;i++){pt.userData.runRig.legs[i].rotation.x=Math.sin(stride+i*Math.PI)*.60;pt.userData.runRig.arms[i].rotation.x=-Math.sin(stride+i*Math.PI)*.72-.18}
 if(a.t>=7.8&&a.t<9.25){const reach=clamp((a.t-7.8)/.45,0,1);pt.position.set(1200.12+reach*.70,0,-21);pt.rotation.x*=Math.exp(-dt*10);pt.rotation.z*=Math.exp(-dt*10);pt.rotation.y+=Math.atan2(Math.sin(-pt.rotation.y),Math.cos(-pt.rotation.y))*(1-Math.exp(-dt*9));pt.userData.head.rotation.y=-.18;for(const j of pt.userData.runRig.legs)j.rotation.x=0;pt.userData.runRig.arms[0].rotation.x=-.95*reach;pt.userData.runRig.arms[1].rotation.x=-.25;}
 if(a.t<7.9){a.step+=dt;if(a.step>.29){a.step=0;He.step(true)}}
 if(a.t>=8.2&&!a.closing){a.closing=true;setDoorOpen('corridor',false);}
 if(a.closing&&!doors.corridor.motion&&Math.abs(doors.corridor.pivot.rotation.y)<.02&&!a.slammed){a.slammed=true;a.slamAt=a.t;He.slam();shake=.65;si.uniforms.uPulse.value=.28}
 if(a.slammed&&a.t>=9.25&&!a.latched){a.latched=true;pt.visible=false;He.click(.4)}
 if(a.slammed){ct.position.z+=Math.sin((a.t-(a.slamAt||8.7))*31)*Math.exp(-(a.t-(a.slamAt||8.7))*7)*.055;ct.rotation.x+=Math.sin(a.t*51)*shake*.024}
}
function finishDanuChase(){
 const a=danuChase;if(!a)return;
 if(!a.slammed){He.slam();shake=.45}
 danuChase=null;resetDanuRunPose();pt.visible=false;setDoorOpen('corridor',false,true);
 player={x:1200,z:-17.55,yaw:ct.rotation.y,pitch:clamp(ct.rotation.x,-1.25,1.25)};ct.position.set(player.x,1.62,player.z);
 state.flags.exitLocked=true;state.flags.danuChaseSeen=true;state.sanity=clamp(state.sanity-5,1,100);
 He.setBreath(0);saveProgress();updateHUD();subtitle('Terkunci dari luar. Dari sambungan logam di timur lorong terasa hembusan udara.',6);
}
function transition(action,duration=.55){if(fade)return;cancelToolReach();clearKeys();unlock();fade={time:0,duration,action,done:false};$('fader').style.transition='none';}

function resetNpcs(){resetDanuRunPose();for(const [name,npc]of Object.entries(npcMap))setNpcExpression(name,npc.userData.faceDefault);pt.userData.head.rotation.y=0;pt.rotation.set(0,Math.PI/2,0);pt.position.set(-1.28,0,-11.5);pt.userData.head.rotation.x=0;kn.position.set(.85,0,-10.4);kn.rotation.y=-Math.PI/2;Bn.position.set(.5,0,-12.1);Bn.rotation.y=-Math.PI/2;Sn.position.set(.5,0,-19);Sn.rotation.y=Math.PI;}
function setRoom(id,restore=false){MechanicTutorial.cancel();resetWatcherSightings();officeExitActor=null;resetContextUI();cancelDaisyCredits();cancelDevour();cancelLenteraCinema();StudentChat.close();Voice.stop();Voice.clearNote();cancelHandsPursuit();cancelDanuReversion();cancelDanuChase();warehouseEntry=null;clearFight();state.room=id;normaliseNarrative(state);normaliseExploration(state);normalisePhotoEvidence(state);syncExplorationDoors(true);syncPassageDoors(true);const room=rooms[id];Object.values(rooms).forEach(r=>r.group.visible=r.id===id);At.visible=false;Ee.visible=false;doll.visible=id==='daisy';doll.position.y=.62;doll.rotation.y=0;crateLid.rotation.set(0,0,0);dollEyelids.forEach(lid=>lid.visible=id==='daisy');resetNpcs();baseNpcs.forEach(n=>n.visible=id==='school');bi=id;rc.position.set(room.cx,0,0);const p=restore&&state.position?state.position:room.spawn;player={x:p.x,z:p.z,yaw:p.yaw||0,pitch:clamp(p.pitch||0,-1.2,1.2)};if(collides(player.x,player.z))player={...room.spawn,pitch:0};ct.position.set(player.x,(id==='pursuit'?pursuitEyeHeight(player.x,player.z):1.62)+stairFloor(player.x,player.z),player.z);ct.rotation.set(player.pitch,player.yaw,0);flashlight=id!=='school';resetLampPose();beamHeat=beamCooldown=0;if(!restore)state.sanity=clamp(state.sanity+(id==='corridor'?25:18),0,100);ambientClock=10;

 applyReadableRoomProfile(id);discoverySun.shadow.needsUpdate=true;

 applyToolStory(restore);updateHUD();saveProgress();
 if(id==='loading'&&!state.flags.loadingIntro){state.flags.loadingIntro=true;runSequence([{text:'Pintu bangsal menghantam kusennya.  if(id==='pursuit')queueAfterFade(()=>startHandsPursuit(restore));

let deferredAfterFade=null;function queueAfterFade(fn){if(fade)deferredAfterFade=fn;else fn()}
function objective(){const f=state.flags,p=state.puzzles;switch(state.room){case 'school':return !state.evidence.A?'Bicara dengan Sari dan Adit. Buka loker Raka di koridor barat.':!f.invited?'Bicarakan surat itu dengan Danu. Periksa bukti lain sebelum pulang.':'Ikuti undangan Danu melalui gerbang selatan. Bukti tambahan tersedia di kelas 1A.';case 'street':return !p.fuse.solved?'Cari agenda Sari di warung. Pulihkan sekering di sisi barat jalan.':'Lampu gang menyala. Masuk gudang di ujung utara Jalan Kartini.';case 'loading':return !p.panel.solved?'Pulihkan panel kendali bangsal. Periksa arsip dan peralatan di ruang samping.':!f.overseerDefeated?'SI PENGAWAS · bertahan dan putuskan tugas jaganya.':'Ada ruang lain di balik pintu utara.';case 'relay':return !f.lenteraOwned?'Telusuri rumah relai.':!f.relaySolved?'Pintu arsip masih tertutup.':'Pengunci arsip telah lepas.';case 'pursuit':return state.flags.extendedPursuitStage?'Ikuti ventilasi menurun. Tarik pengunci dan rem kipas dengan penjepit.':'Lari melalui jalur pemeliharaan. Cari akses servis di ujung saluran.';case 'production':return !p.valve.solved?'Periksa sistem dan perlengkapan tekanan di ruang produksi.':!f.handsDefeated?'TANGAN-TANGAN · putuskan cengkeramannya.':'Saluran aman. Masuk kantor melalui pintu utara.';case 'office':return !state.evidence.B?'Baca album dan perjanjian di meja Danu.':!p.breaker.solved?'Periksa arsip kantor dan rekaman audit sebelum membuka ruang ritual.':'Danu masuk ruang ritual di utara. Jalur servis di selatan masih tersedia.';case 'ritual':return !f.tumbalDefeated?'PEMEGANG · nama-nama yang dihapus masih mengikat perjanjian.':'Perjanjian runtuh. Masuk lorong pemeliharaan di utara.';case 'corridor':return !f.exitLocked?'Kejar Danu menuju pintu keluar di ujung lorong.':'Danu mengunci pintu utama. Cari pintu logam D–08 di dinding timur.';case 'daisy':return 'Periksa lemari kaca bertuliskan DAISY.';default:return ''}}
let lastHudSignature='';
function updateHUD(){const text=objective(),signature=state.room+'|'+text+'|'+Object.keys(state.evidence).filter(k=>state.evidence[k]).join(',')+'|'+Math.ceil(state.sanity)+'|'+Math.floor(state.elapsed);if(signature===lastHudSignature)return;lastHudSignature=signature;$('objective').textContent=text;$('evdList').innerHTML=[['A','surat loker'],['op1','sobekan foto'],['op2','agenda Sari'],['B','album & perjanjian']].map(([k,v])=>`<span class="${state.evidence[k]?'got':''}">${state.evidence[k]?'■':'□'} ${v}</span>`).join('');$('sanityFill').style.width=state.sanity+'%';$('sanityValue').textContent=Math.ceil(state.sanity);$('sanityFill').style.background=state.sanity<28?'#c6594f':'#cfc8b6';$('damage').style.opacity=state.sanity<28?.35+(28-state.sanity)/70:0;$('playTime').textContent=formatTime(state.elapsed)}
function formatTime(s){return Math.floor(s/60).toString().padStart(2,'0')+':'+Math.floor(s%60).toString().padStart(2,'0')}
function saveProgress(show=false){if(daisyCredits||devour||daisyRescue||!saveAllowed||['title','ending'].includes(state.room))return;state.position={...player};checkpoint=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state));try{localStorage.setItem(saveKey,JSON.stringify(checkpoint));$('saveIndicator').textContent='Tersimpan';$('saveIndicator').classList.add('show');setSaveAge=2.2;storageAvailable=true;if(show)toast('Progres tersimpan')}catch{storageAvailable=false;$('saveIndicator').textContent='CHECKPOINT SESI';if(show)toast('Penyimpanan browser dibatasi. Checkpoint tersedia selama tab ini terbuka.')}updateContinue()}

function readSave(){try{const s=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(saveKey)||localStorage.getItem(legacySaveKey)||'null');if(!s||s.version!==saveVersion||!roomOrder.includes(s.room)||!s.puzzles||!s.flags||!s.evidence||!Array.isArray(s.notes)||!s.seen||!Number.isFinite(s.sanity)||!Number.isFinite(s.elapsed))return null;for(const [id,p]of Object.entries(freshState().puzzles)){const q=s.puzzles[id];if(!q||typeof q.solved!=='boolean')return null;if('entered'in p&&typeof q.entered!=='string')return null;if('current'in p&&(!Array.isArray(q.current)||q.current.length>4))return null;if('pos'in p&&(!Array.isArray(q.pos)||q.pos.length!==3||q.pos.some(n=>!Number.isInteger(n)||n<0||n>3)))return null}if(s.flags.contentRevision!==2){if(['production','office','ritual','corridor','daisy'].includes(s.room)||s.flags.handsDefeated){s.flags.lenteraOwned=true;s.flags.oldFlashBroken=true;s.flags.flashBreakComplete=true;s.flags.relaySolved=true;s.flags.relayPower=true;s.flags.relayRotors=[2,1,3]}s.flags.contentRevision=2}if(!Array.isArray(s.flags.relayRotors)||s.flags.relayRotors.length!==3||s.flags.relayRotors.some(v=>!Number.isInteger(v)||v<0||v>3))s.flags.relayRotors=[0,0,0];if(s.position&&(!Number.isFinite(s.position.x)||!Number.isFinite(s.position.z)||!Number.isFinite(s.position.yaw)))return null;normaliseExploration(s);normaliseNarrative(s);normalisePhotoEvidence(s);migrateSchoolDialogues(s);return s}catch{return null}}

// Three authored phases. Each phase starts with three candles, three claw hits each.
const RITUAL_INTERVAL=12,ASH_GROWTH_INTERVAL=5;
const finalSeals=[];

}
const ritualHUD=document.createElement('div');ritualHUD.id='ritualHUD';ritualHUD.style.cssText='display:none;position:fixed;top:121px;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);width:min(440px,70vw);padding:8px 12px;text-align:center;background:rgba(4,9,8,.78);color:#d8c4a1;font:11px monospace;line-height:1.7;pointer-events:none;z-index:12;border-top:1px solid #775839';document.body.append(ritualHUD);

function makeAshMinion(index){
 const mesh=new It();mesh.name='Juru_Abu_'+index;Wt.add(mesh);
 const coat=new Xe({color:0x48423a,roughness:.98}),mask=new Xe({color:0xa5a18e,roughness:.94}),charcoal=material(0x181d1a),ember=new Cn({color:0xe5ad60});
 const body=new It();mesh.add(body);const robe=new ae(new vi(.40,1.03,7),coat);robe.position.y=.73;body.add(robe);
 for(let j=0;j<4;j++)box(body,0,.55+j*.13,.23,.38,.025,.045,mats.rust);
 const head=joint(body,0,1.28,.06);ellipsoid(head,0,0,0,.18,.24,.17,mask,16,12);box(head,0,-.015,.168,.055,.30,.025,charcoal);box(head,0,.045,.185,.017,.08,.012,ember);
 for(const side of [-1,1]){limb(body,[side*.28,1.03,0],[side*.43,.57,.17],.065,.04,mats.rust,8);limb(body,[side*.43,.57,.17],[side*.33,.39,.34],.045,.025,mask,8);box(body,side*.33,.35,.35,.055,.14,.08,charcoal);}
 batchDollPieces(body);const legs=[];for(const side of [-1,1]){const leg=joint(mesh,side*.15,.28,0);limb(leg,[0,0,0],[0,-.20,.06],.075,.04,charcoal,8);ellipsoid(leg,0,-.22,.10,.09,.055,.16,mats.rust,12,8);batchDollPieces(leg);legs.push(leg);}
 const bar=new ae(new _t(.7,.045),new Cn({color:0xd7a065,side:xn}));bar.position.y=1.75;mesh.add(bar);mesh.visible=false;
 mesh.traverse(m=>{if(m.isMesh){m.castShadow=!m.material.transparent;m.receiveShadow=true;}});
 return {mesh,body,legs,bar,active:false,hp:30,maxHp:30,age:0,growth:0,hitTimer:1,stagger:0};
}
// A small pool keeps repeated ritual summons bounded and reuses geometry.
const ashMinions=[makeAshMinion(0),makeAshMinion(1)];
function clearAshMinions(){for(const m of ashMinions){m.active=false;m.mesh.visible=false;}ritualHUD.style.display='none';}
function summonAshMinion(f){
 const m=ashMinions.find(m=>!m.active);if(!m)return false;
 const points=[[994,-10],[1007,6],[1006,-10],[994,5]],start=f.summonCount||0;let point=points[start%points.length];
 for(let i=0;i<points.length;i++){const p=points[(start+i)%points.length];if(!collides(p[0],p[1],.45)&&Math.hypot(player.x-p[0],player.z-p[1])>3){point=p;break;}}
 Object.assign(m,{active:true,hp:30,maxHp:30,age:0,growth:0,hitTimer:1.5,stagger:0});m.mesh.position.set(point[0],-.12,point[1]);m.mesh.visible=true;f.summonCount=start+1;He.creak();return true;
}
function damageAshMinion(m,amount){
 if(!m.active||!currentFight||currentFight.kind!=='tumbal')return;
 m.hp=Math.max(0,m.hp-amount);m.stagger=.14;
 if(m.hp<=0){m.active=false;m.mesh.visible=false;He.vanish();toast('Juru Abu runtuh.');}
}
function aimedAshMinion(){
 ct.getWorldDirection(lookVector);let best=null,bestDistance=Infinity;
 for(const m of ashMinions){if(!m.active)continue;const point=m.mesh.position.clone().add(new I(0,1.05,0)),delta=point.clone().sub(ct.position),distance=delta.length();if(distance<17&&distance<bestDistance&&lookVector.dot(delta.normalize())>.955&&clearToolLine(point)){best=m;bestDistance=distance;}}
 return best;
}
function beginRitualPhase(f,phase){
 f.phase=phase;f.shield=true;f.sealBroken=0;f.ritualCountdown=RITUAL_INTERVAL;f.ritualPulses=0;f.recovery=phase===1?1.8:2.2;f.dashTimer=4;f.coreClock=0;f.coreOpen=false;f.teleportTimer=3;
 for(const s of finalSeals){s.hp=3;s.broken=false;s.lastHit=-Infinity;s.mesh.visible=true;}
 $('bossPhase').textContent=`Fase ${phase}/3`;
 He.vanish();shake=.24;if(phase===1)hintOnce('candles','R  Tarik tiap lilin tiga kali',true);
}
function breakFinalSeal(s){
 const f=currentFight;if(f?.kind!=='tumbal'||!f.shield||s.hp<=0||now-s.lastHit<.30)return;
 s.lastHit=now;s.hp--;s.broken=s.hp===0;He.click(.3);shake=.09;
 if(s.broken){f.sealBroken++;state.sanity=clamp(state.sanity+4,0,100);He.vanish();}
 // Remaining candle integrity is shown on its current interaction prompt.
 if(finalSeals.every(s=>s.broken)){f.shield=false;f.recovery=2.5;f.dashTimer=4;f.coreClock=0;f.coreOpen=true;subtitle('',4);}
}
function updateRitual(dt){
 const f=currentFight;if(f?.kind!=='tumbal'){ritualHUD.style.display='none';return;}
 if(f.shield&&finalSeals.some(s=>s.hp>0)){
  f.ritualCountdown-=dt;
  while(f.ritualCountdown<=0){f.ritualCountdown+=RITUAL_INTERVAL;const heal=Math.min(f.maxHp-f.hp,f.maxHp*.20);f.hp+=heal;f.ritualPulses++;const summoned=summonAshMinion(f);f.castPose=1;MonsterVocal.urge();hintOnce('ritual_heal','Api menyala. Ritual memulihkannya.',true);}
 }
 for(const m of ashMinions){if(!m.active)continue;m.age+=dt;m.growth+=dt;m.hitTimer-=dt;m.stagger=Math.max(0,m.stagger-dt);
  while(m.growth>=ASH_GROWTH_INTERVAL){m.growth-=ASH_GROWTH_INTERVAL;m.maxHp+=2;m.hp+=2;}
  const dx=player.x-m.mesh.position.x,dz=player.z-m.mesh.position.z,d=Math.max(.01,Math.hypot(dx,dz));
  if(d>1.05&&m.stagger<=0)moveEntity(m.mesh,dx/d*1.65*dt,dz/d*1.65*dt,.33);
  m.mesh.position.y=Math.min(0,m.mesh.position.y+dt*.4);m.mesh.lookAt(player.x,m.mesh.position.y,player.z);m.body.rotation.x=.16+Math.sin(m.age*2)*.035;m.body.position.y=Math.abs(Math.sin(m.age*6))*.035;
  m.legs.forEach((leg,i)=>leg.rotation.x=Math.sin(m.age*7+i*Math.PI)*.4);m.bar.scale.x=Math.max(.01,m.hp/m.maxHp);m.bar.quaternion.copy(m.mesh.quaternion).invert().multiply(ct.quaternion);
  if(d<1.15&&m.hitTimer<=0){m.hitTimer=1.8;hurt(6);if(!currentFight)return;}
 }
}
function updateRitualVisual(){
 const f=currentFight,fighting=f?.kind==='tumbal';
 ritualHUD.style.display=fighting?'block':'none';
 if(fighting){ritualHUD.textContent=f.shield?`Lilin  I ${finalSeals[0].hp}/3  ·  II ${finalSeals[1].hp}/3  ·  III ${finalSeals[2].hp}/3   |   ${Math.max(0,Math.ceil(f.ritualCountdown))} dtk`:'Api padam';ritualHUD.style.whiteSpace='pre-line';}
 for(const s of finalSeals){s.mesh.visible=fighting;if(!fighting)continue;s.flame.visible=s.glow.visible=s.hp>0;s.wax.scale.y=.64+.36*s.hp/3;const top=1.16+.46*s.wax.scale.y;s.wax.position.y=1.16+.23*s.wax.scale.y;s.wick.position.y=top+.03;s.flame.position.y=s.glow.position.y=top+.16;s.flame.scale.y=.145*(.92+Math.sin(now*13+s.index)*.08);s.glow.scale.y=.22*(.9+Math.sin(now*9+s.index)*.1);s.pips.forEach((m,i)=>m.visible=i<s.hp);}
}
function advanceRitualPhase(f){
 if(f.kind!=='tumbal'||f.shield||f.phase>=3)return;
 const threshold=f.maxHp*(f.phase===1?2/3:1/3);
 if(f.hp<=threshold){f.hp=threshold;beginRitualPhase(f,f.phase+1);}
}

// Soft, irregular smoke sprites: bounded billboards, not full-room fog or bloom.
const smokeCanvas=document.createElement('canvas');smokeCanvas.width=smokeCanvas.height=128;const sm=smokeCanvas.getContext('2d');
let smokeSeed=6173;const smokeRand=()=>((smokeSeed=(Math.imul(smokeSeed,1664525)+1013904223)>>>0)/4294967296);
for(let i=0;i<34;i++){const x=29+smokeRand()*70,y=29+smokeRand()*70,r=14+smokeRand()*23,g=sm.createRadialGradient(x,y,1,x,y,r);g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,255,255,.11)');g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,255,255,0)');sm.fillStyle=g;sm.fillRect(x-r,y-r,r*2,r*2);}
const smokeTexture=new Ms(smokeCanvas);smokeTexture.needsUpdate=true;const smokePlane=new _t(1,1);
function smokeSprite(parent,mat,x,y,z,size,seed){const m=new ae(smokePlane,mat);m.position.set(x,y,z);m.scale.set(size,size*.78,1);m.userData.base=[x,y,z];m.userData.seed=seed;m.userData.noGrip=true;m.renderOrder=3;parent.add(m);return m;}
function disposeCombatNode(root){if(!root)return;root.removeFromParent();for(const mat of root.userData.disposableMaterials||[])mat.dispose();}
